# Route inventory 2026-11-02

Generated from a read-only source-tracing pass (8 subsystem inventories plus a completeness critic) and cross-checked against native 480 x 800 captures of the first-level legacy routes. Status is "Confirmed gap" for legacy/mixed routes with findings and "Not audited (render pending)" otherwise; nothing here is simulator- or device-verified unless `UI_AUDIT.md` says so. Evidence references are `file:line` at the time of tracing and may drift.

## shell

| ID / journey | Entry / source | Presentation | Findings | Required R1 behavior | Spotify counterpart | Status |

**Shared components:**

- gui/shell/theme.c palette: the dark palette is Adwaita (#242424 screen, #303030 surface, default accent blue #3584e4; theme.c:24-33, 69-77). Adding a reference-layout palette (#121212 bg, #282828 raised, #202124 sheet, #b3b3b3 secondary, #1ed760 accent) would re-skin every theme_style_* consumer at once: gridpage, settingsrow, confirm, toast, popover, quickpanel, welcome/language/setupwifi/timeset, volume HUD, keyboard keys, topbar.
- gui/shell/settingsrow.c (about 55 files): settingsrow_page/title/add/make_card define the legacy header (font_ui_32 title beside the floating chevron) and #303030 card rows. Converting it to a Spotify header plus flat list rows, with optional footer mounting, fixes Settings, Wireless, More, Music settings, audio and device pages together.
- gui/shell/gridpage.c: used by tools_screen, music, wireless, audiobooks and morepage. Replacing the tile grid with Spotify list rows or a bottom-sheet launcher removes five legacy hubs at once. The empty and no-card panels (gridpage.c:226-354) need Spotify empty-state styling.
- gui/shell/main_menu.c main_menu_mount_footer/restore_footer: only medialist and discography call these. Calling them from switcher load_screen (or a page flag) would give every shell descendant the mini-player and nav and remove the chevron-only escape route.
- gui/shell/confirm.c + gui/shell/toast.c + gui.c popup/modal: three legacy notice systems. Unify them into one Spotify alert dialog and one bottom snackbar used by about 120 call sites.
- gui/shell/popover.c: about 22 consumers of an Adwaita popover. Route them through bottomsheet.c with standard menu rows for Spotify context and sort menus.
- gui/shell/topbar.c + switcher.c tint slots: single-slot tint_screen/chevron_tint_screen are overwritten by medialist (medialist.c:4525-4531), so the main menu's #121212 bar is lost. A per-screen tint registry or a reference default fixes the status-bar mismatch.
- gui/shell/switcher.c back button: a global floating chevron. Folding it into a shared Spotify page header component (with title) aligns every legacy page.
- gui/shell/keyboard.c kb_make_key: keys use theme_style_card. Applying the raised token in the reference layout, and switching the layout chooser from popover to a sheet, fixes every keyboard consumer.
- gui/shell/ui_probe.c: route reporting is the shared test hook for verifying all of the above.

**Probe/test notes:** Current probe limits: ui_probe.c:102 reports route only as 'collection' (medialist_tracks_screen), 'main' or 'other', so tools_screen, settings_screen, wireless_screen, morepage_screen, browser_screen, search_screen, music_screen and libraryscan_screen are indistinguishable. ui_probe.c:103 'modal' reflects only bottomsheet_active(). The quickpanel, power menu, confirm, toast, popup, gui modal, popover, screensaver, welcome, language, setupwifi and timeset overlays are not flagged; they can only be inferred from lv_layer_top nodes (walked at ui_probe.c:119). Probe setup commands exist only for collection-find, search-text, keyboard-refresh, playlist and album (ui_probe.c:76-90). tools/offline_ui_session.py:14 supports only start, click, drag, shot and stop, with no key action. What can be done today: - Header vertical-ellipsis: click at about (382, top_bar+34). Gear: click at about (438, top_bar+34). - Legacy grid tiles have text labels, so click_label('Wireless'), click_label('More') and similar work. - Quickpanel: drag from (240, 20) to (240, 500). - Back: click the chevron at about (padding+28, top_bar+padding+28), or drag rightward from x=60. Missing probe commands to add: - 'screen' returning a named route for every lv_obj_t in gui.c:582-623 plus tools_screen (export a getter from main_menu.c). - 'open-tools' and 'open-settings'. - 'quickpanel-open' / 'quickpanel-close' with a quickpanel_is_open() flag. - 'powermenu' with a powermenu_is_open() flag. - 'confirm-sample', 'toast-sample' and 'popup-sample', each with a visible flag. - 'screensaver-show' with screensaver_is_visible(). - 'first-boot' (welcome_show_first_boot), 'setupwifi' and 'timeset'. - 'history' dumping screen_history depth and names. - A 'key <p\|u\|i\|b\|n\|m> <down\|up\|tap\|hold ms>' action in offline_ui_session.py using xdotool keydown/keyup (host maps these to KEY_POWER and the others at main.c:1101-1124) to exercise the power menu, screen toggle and transport. Tests to add in tools/test_ui_collections.py: - A 'shell-escapes' journey that taps the vertical-ellipsis, the gear, each tools tile, Search 'Files & folders' and 'See all results'. It should assert route != legacy and that Home/Search/Your Library footer labels are visible. These currently fail by design and document the gap. - A status-bar check: open an album, return Home, and sample pixel (10,10) == #121212. capture() currently paints rows 0-43 #121212, which hides this regression, so it must sample before normalization. - Repeated entry/exit of tools > wireless > bluetooth > back x N, checking history depth and returning to main. - Restart persistence of ui.last_tab, which is already reported in probe 'tab'. - Quickpanel toggle persistence (screen.brightness, quickpanel.order). - Low-battery and confirm dialog styling via a probe sample command. Hardware-only items: power long press (POWER_LONG_PRESS_MS), the double-tap wake, vol+power combos, headset hook clicks and screen-off/screensaver wake timing cannot be verified on the host.

(appearance.c:172-176 (pick_cb -> theme_set_dark); main_menu.c:86, 659, 712 (if(theme_is_dark()) one-time local 0x121212); theme.c:42-51 (light text_primary 50,50,50); no theme_register_refresh in main_menu.c)<br>**major**: The accent picker offers six Adwaita accents with no Spotify #1ed760; blue is the default. A settingsrow restyle will not reach the hand-built cards. (appearance.c:155-193, 199-300; settingsrow.c:890-905)<br>**polish**: Each theme or accent change triggers bootlogo_follow, which re-writes the NAND theme marker (flash_erase/nandwrite on /dev/mtd5) on a background thread. **Shared components:**

- gui/shell/settingsrow.c/.h. This is the row and page kit for about 55 files: every settings page here plus the eq/mseb/peq settings, btsettings, btaudio, gearboysettings, audiobooks options, ebooksettings, musicsettings, wifisettings and others. API: settingsrow_page (adds theme_style_screen, a 32 px title beside the floating chevron, and a flex column container from settingsrow_content_top = top_bar+padding+56+padding to screen_height with an 8 px gap; attaches swipe-back; scroll-to-top on unload). settingsrow_title (32 to 24 px step-down, one line, registered for text-size refit, max 160). settingsrow_title_corner_slots / settingsrow_page_title. settingsrow_add (100 px lv_btn with theme_style_card and theme_style_card_pressed, radius 12, pad 20, 24 px name with fit_row_label 24/22/20, optional dim 24 px value or a 60%-opacity chevron, click shim that ignores swipe drags). settingsrow_action (no chevron). settingsrow_heading (dim 20 px bold). settingsrow_chevron_active (Adwaita green #2ec27e). settingsrow_name_label / settingsrow_name_lines. settingsrow_slider (140 px card, 10 px track with theme_style_slider_track, accent indicator, white knob with shadow, white step ticks up to 16 steps). settingsrow_toggle (100 px card with a 68x36 switch). settingsrow_switch (theme_style_switch / theme_style_switch_checked = accent; tap-only via CLICKED; 24 px extended hit area). settingsrow_toggle_slider and _expanded (100 to 180 px). settingsrow_toggle_pills / settingsrow_pills (padded content-sized card with a wrap row of pills). settingsrow_pill / settingsrow_pill_text (56 px circle-radius buttons, 22 px text) and settingsrow_pill_active (accent with white text, or surface_pressed with text_primary). settingsrow_toggle_duration and its wheels (lv_roller with accent selected text). Restyling settingsrow_page/add/make_card/switch/slider/pill to reference tokens would convert most settings rows product-wide in one change: #121212 page, flat or #121212 rows of 64-72 px, white 18-20 px labels, #b3b3b3 values, green #1ed760 switches and active chips with black ink, and a bold reference header. It would not reach the hand-built widgets: the settings.c brightness card and QWERTY/T9 pills, appearance.c's theme/clock/accent cards, the systempage SD card, processespage cards, kblayoutpage and ccsettings rows, timeset panels, remap rows and dialog, and the language/bootlogo check marks. Those must migrate to the kit first. settingsrow_page should also gain an optional reference footer (main_menu_mount_footer) and reduce container height to clear it.
- gui/shell/theme.c. palette_dark is Adwaita (#242424 screen, #303030 cards, #a0a0a0 secondary, blue #3584e4 default accent), and every theme_style_* consumer inherits it. Changing palette_dark to reference tokens (#121212 screen, #282828 or #1f1f1f surface, #b3b3b3 secondary, #1ed760 accent), or locking accent=green and dark when player_layout_alt==3, fixes colour fidelity across the whole settings tree at once. The Light theme, accent and dynamic-tint options in Appearance must then be hidden in the reference layout, or the shell must register theme refresh, because the shell hard-codes #121212/#1ed760 (main_menu.c:86/659/712, keyboard.c:31, homeview.c, medialist.c).
- gui/shell/switcher.c back_btn (one floating top-layer chevron for all pages) plus load_screen/history. This is the shared header back affordance; restyling or replacing it with a reference header bar affects every page. Top-layer panels (timeset, language first-boot, tz list, remap double dialog, confirm, popover) cover it and bypass history and swipe-back.
- gui/shell/confirm.c (confirm_show/confirm_notice), the gui.c notice popup (gui_notify_popup) and gui/shell/popover.c. These are legacy centred cards on a 60% veil with accent buttons, used by factory reset, the screensaver empty notice, the Wi-Fi enable prompt, devoptions unlock popups and the remap chooser. Migrating them to bottomsheet.c, or to a reference dialog with pill buttons and destructive styling, fixes all settings dialogs together.
- gui/shell/bottomsheet.c. This is the reference sheet (#202124, radius 22, drag handle, close, 150-opacity scrim, UI_SHEET_TRANSITION_MS, one modal layer). It is unused by any settings route and should host option choosers (remap actions, durations, screensaver source, keyboard type, clock position, time zone).
- gui/shell/main_menu.c main_menu_mount_footer and topbar_tint_on_screen. Exposing these for settings screens gives the mini-player and nav plus a #121212 status bar throughout the tree.
- gui/shell/main_menu.c label() and the homeview/collectionview strings. The lack of tr() in the shell makes SET-LANGUAGE produce a mixed-language product; this is a shared i18n fix.

**Probe/test notes:** Probe gaps in ui_probe.c: the route field reports only 'collection', 'main' or 'other' (ui_probe.c:102), so every settings page is 'other'. Add route names by comparing lv_screen_active() against settings_screen, appearance_screen, language_screen, powersettings_screen, systempage_screen, sysinfo_screen, devoptions_screen, processespage_screen, bootlogopage_screen, remap_screen, ccsettings_screen, kblayoutpage_screen and tools_screen. The Screen, Keys and Keyboard pages are static in settings.c (screensettings_screen, othersettings_screen, keyboard_screen); export getters for them. The 'modal' field reflects only bottomsheet_active(), so also report visibility of the timeset panel, the tz panel, the confirm veil, the gui popup veil, the popover veil, the remap double_veil, the screensaver and the language first-boot panel. Report theme_is_dark(), theme_get_accent(), theme_dynamic_tint() and lang_current() so persistence checks do not need pixel reads. The node walk already includes lv_layer_top, so overlay contents are inspectable. Setup commands to add (setup-only, per ui_probe.c:75): 'screen <name>' (switch_screen to a named settings page), 'timeset' (timeset_show), 'theme dark\|light' and 'accent <n>' (exposes the light-theme contrast bug). Harness gaps in tools/test_ui_collections.py: there is no settings journey (choices at test_ui_collections.py:339). Proposed --journey settings. From Home, click header_settings (60x48 at x=408, y=top_bar+10; locate the clickable node of that size). Capture settings-root and assert the background pixel is about #121212 (RGB565 is about #101010, as in capture()); this currently FAILS at #242424 and exposes the gap. Assert the Home/Search/Your Library labels are present for footer persistence (currently absent). Click each row label (Appearance, Screen, Language/..., Keys, Control centre, Keyboard, Date and time, Power, System), capture, then click the top-layer chevron (56x56 clickable node at padding, top_bar+padding) and assert a return to settings. Repeat entry and exit twice while audio plays and assert queue/status unchanged in the probe response. Toggle 'Battery percentage' and move 'Screen off', restart with --preserve-config (as at test_ui_collections.py:176) and assert the state persists. Open Date and time with a set clock and assert the cancel chevron is visible and closes; with an unset clock the expectation should be 'cancellable', which encodes the major finding. Factory reset: open confirm 1, then cancel only (HOST_BUILD prints and does not reboot; never run on device without authorization). Developer options: five clicks on 'Build number' in About; assert the root row appears. Remap: host photos may be missing (side_load prints 'missing'), so treat an empty photo as a fixture limitation. Also add a tools journey asserting the ellipsis does not open a gridpage (currently it does, main_menu.c:646-655). Captures are candidates, not baselines; host timing and RSS are not R1 measurements. Factory reset, bootlogo NAND writes, PMIC charge, LED and ADB effects are hardware-only. Spotify reference used: https://support.spotify.com/us/article/internet-and-data-usage/ and https://www.slashgear.com/1965140/how-to-open-spotify-settings-on-android (accessed 2026-11-02; no dated official section list found, so mark the Settings layout research as medium confidence and needing a dated mobile capture).

**Shared components:**

- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/settingsrow.c (settingsrow_page/settingsrow_title/make_card/toggle/pill/slider/duration): every Music settings subpage, artistexceptions, musictiles, Last.fm, and the titles of browser, filespage, textview, imageview, libraryscan, playlistpage and search are built with it. Adding a Spotify mode here (#121212 page, header row with back chevron and a 22-24px bold title, flat #121212 rows or #282828 groups, green #1ed760 switches/pills/sliders, #b3b3b3 descriptions) fixes about 30 routes in this area at once.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/theme.c palette_dark (#242424/#303030/#3584e4): theme_style_screen/card/accent_bg/text_dim drive the legacy look of browser rows, filespage rows, the scan picker, empty panels, popovers and confirms. A reference palette tied to player_layout_alt==3 would cascade, but needs a check against the main_menu/medialist overrides (main_menu.c:84, medialist.c:4531).
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/main_menu.c tools_screen grid (646-655) and gear (667-672 -> settings_screen): replacing the ⋮ legacy gridpage with a Spotify-style 'Settings and more' surface, and adding Music/Playback/Library/Local files groups to the gear's Settings tree, removes the legacy-only reachability of Music settings and Scan.
- sonix-player/src/gui/library/libraryscan.c back_to_music (67-70) and switcher.c load_screen (505-512): send scan completion back to the originating screen (or Your Library), and make libraryscan_begin idempotent while scan_running. That fixes the legacy handoff and the false 'no card' state on re-entry.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/bottomsheet.c: should replace the legacy popover.c (filespage row menu), the confirm.c dialogs (filespage delete, MSEB reset, Last.fm sign-out), the libraryscan folder-picker veil, and the playlistpage_add_tracks legacy picker used by browser selection.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/gridpage.c (gridpage_build, empty and no-card panels): used by the tools grid, classic Music, More and empty states. Retiring or restyling it covers several entry points.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/keyboard.c: already reference-styled under player_layout_alt==3 (keyboard.c:30-32). The name layers hosting it (filespage, artistexceptions, Last.fm login) are legacy, so a shared Spotify name-entry layer would make them consistent.
- main_menu_mount_footer (main_menu.c:592-598): only medialist and discography mount the mini-player/nav. Browser and settings pages reached from the shell should mount it, or be sheets.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/toast.c and gui_notify_popup: Adwaita green/red toasts and popups used by browser, filespage, scan and Last.fm. A Spotify snackbar style here fixes the feedback in all of them.
- playback services: the browser's player_play_file + switch_screen(player_screen) pattern (browser.c:913-914, 1004-1005) should go through the shared preview-mode play path used by collections. Move playcount_note_playback out of lastfmsettings.c tick_cb (309-322) into a playback service.

**Probe/test notes:** Current tooling cannot open or identify most of these routes: (1) The route detector is too coarse. ui_probe.c probe_poll reports route only as 'collection' (medialist_tracks_screen), 'main' or 'other', so music_screen, browser_screen, filespage_screen, textview_screen, imageview_screen, libraryscan_screen, musicsettings_screen and its static subpages, artistexceptions_screen, musictiles_screen and the Last.fm page/login all read as 'other'. Add a screen-name table, e.g. extern pointers plus accessor getters for the static scan_screen/library_screen/nowplaying_screen/playback_screen/fade_screen in musicsettings.c, and report whether the libraryscan pick_veil (lv_layer_top) is visible. (2) There are no open commands. Probe commands exist only for collection-find, search-text, keyboard-refresh, playlist and album. Add setup-only commands such as 'open tools', 'open music', 'open browser [path]' (browser_open_dir_public), 'open files [path]', 'open music-settings[/scan\|library\|nowplaying\|playback\|fade\|lastfm]', 'scan-choose' (libraryscan_choose_folders) and 'scan-state' (library_scan_running, library_scan_found). Opening 'quickpanel' via quickpanel_open() would let the scan re-entry defect be reproduced. (3) There is no back key. offline_ui_session.py supports only start/click/drag/shot/stop. Add 'back' (back_btn_cb(NULL)) and hardware-key injection so chevron/key back paths (browser_go_up, the filespage back_guard, the libraryscan no-chevron rule) can be exercised. (4) Journeys to add to tools/test_ui_collections.py: - 'search-files': Search tab, tap 'Files & folders', tap a track. Assert now_playing_open stays false (preview mode). Check the pixel at (10,600) is about #121212 rather than Adwaita #242424, and that Home/Search/Your Library labels are visible. - 'scan-import': empty-card fixture, start scan from a shell surface, wait until done, press OK. Assert the route returns to main/Your Library, not music_screen, and assert the DB track count. Repeat with a control-centre long-press mid-scan, then back: the status label must not read 'no card to scan' while scan_running. - 'settings-reachability': the gear must reach Music/Playback/Scan without visiting the tools grid. - 'layout-guard': after selecting a non-Sonix layout pill, assert config player_layout_alt and that the Spotify surfaces are unchanged across restart. - 'files-ops' on a temp card: rename/move an indexed track, assert library.db paths and the queue update. Delete a large folder while audio plays and assert UI responsiveness and an unchanged playback status/file. (5) Pixel checks: capture() asserts the #121212 background only for 'playlist-empty' and 'liked-songs'. Extend it to every captured route in this area so legacy #242424/#303030 surfaces fail visibly. (6) Host RSS/timing from metrics() is not an R1 measurement. The UI-thread I/O defects (filespage read_folder/remove_tree, textview 128 KB read, scan picker stat) need device timing.

**Shared components:**

- gui/shell/settingsrow.c: page/title/row/toggle/slider/pills on the Adwaita theme (theme_style_card, 100 px rows, radius 12, Adwaita green chevron at settingsrow.c:654-664). Every page in this area is built on it, so a Spotify variant (tokens #121212/#282828/#b3b3b3/#1ed760, Spotify back header instead of title plus floating chevron) restyles EQ, PEQ, PEQ band, MSEB, every preset page, DAC filters, the DAC page and the Music settings root together.
- gui/shell/theme.c: palette_dark is Adwaita (#242424 window, #303030 card, accent #3584e4 default, theme.c:23-78). The audio pages color knobs, curves, pills and the metronome from theme()->accent and surface_pressed, so a Spotify palette or accent mapping fixes their colors in one place.
- gui/shell/confirm.c: every reset and delete confirmation (EQ reset, PEQ reset all/band, MSEB reset, preset deletes x3, DAC leave) is the legacy card. Re-skin it as a Spotify dialog or bottomsheet.
- gui/shell/popover.c: the preset vertical-ellipsis menus (eqsettings/msebsettings/peqsettings), the metronome signature picker and the legacy trackmenu all use the legacy popover card. Route them to gui/shell/bottomsheet.c.
- A new shared preset manager to replace three near-identical files (eqsettings.c, msebsettings.c, peqsettings.c: duplicated add_preset_row, name dialog, delete menu). Fix once: sorted and bounded/pooled list, async enumeration, replace-confirm on duplicate names, name layer hidden on unload with back handling, and a Spotify name sheet using gui/shell/keyboard.c.
- system/audio/eq.c persistence: peq_set_band/peq_set_preamp/eq_set_band/eq_set_enabled call config_save synchronously (config.c:249-300). Setter variants that do not save, plus a debounced or on-release save, fix the PEQ per-step writes, the 10x and 11x writes on reset or default preset, and EQ long-press repeat writes.
- corner_button helper duplicated in eqpage.c:489, musicsettings.c:802, peqpage.c:697, dacpage.c:205 and music.c: a shared Spotify header-actions component would also fix the inconsistent gear/reset slot order and the MSEB missing corner-slot reservation.
- gui/shell/main_menu.c tools grid (646-655) and gui/shell/morepage.c (gridpage): replacing them with a Spotify settings/utilities list and adding an Audio entry to settings.c makes EQ, PEQ, MSEB, DAC and Metronome reachable without legacy gridpages.
- gui/shell/quickpanel.c circle buttons (make_circle_button/circle_button_set_on, 1309-1340) for the EQ/MSEB/PEQ toggles.

**Probe/test notes:** ui_probe.c (sonix-player/src/gui/shell/ui_probe.c:73-89) only supports collection-find, search-text, keyboard-refresh, playlist and album setup commands. Its 'route' field reports only collection, main or other (ui_probe.c:105), so no audio screen can be identified. The tree walk covers lv_screen_active() and lv_layer_top() but not lv_layer_sys(). Missing probe commands and fields: (1) a 'route' identity for tools_screen, morepage_screen, music_screen, musicsettings_screen, eqpage_screen(), eqsettings, peqpage_screen(), PEQ band_screen, peqsettings and its list, mseb_screen, msebsettings and its list, dacfilter_screen, dacpage_screen, metronomepage_screen; (2) audio state in the response: eq/peq/mseb enabled, eq band tenths, eq width, peq bands and preamp, mseb range and values, bit_perfect, metronome_running/bpm/beats, usbdac state; (3) visibility of the preset name_layer and PEQ entry_layer plus back-button hidden state, so the stale-dialog defect can be asserted; (4) a config_save counter, so tests can assert one write per gesture (catches the PEQ per-step writes); (5) a quickpanel open/close setup and quickpanel_is_open in the response. tools/test_ui_collections.py has no audio journeys. Add, via s.click_label and SDL pointer: Home vertical ellipsis -> Music -> gear -> Equaliser -> drag a band (assert config value and playback status/seconds unchanged while a track plays), reset confirm, presets apply and auto-return; PEQ -> Band 1 -> drag Frequency (assert config_save count = 1 after release once fixed), keypad entry, preset save, back while naming then re-enter (assert the layer is hidden), duplicate-name save (assert a replace prompt), restart persistence; MSEB slider plus range change; quickpanel tap toggles and long-press open while the player sheet is open (assert the return to the player); metronome refusal while playing and stop on leave. Repeat each entry and exit several times. DAC mode, filters, NOS and gain are hardware-only (host usbdac and ALSA behavior does not prove the R1). Mark them pending device validation. Spotify research used (accessed 2026-11-02): https://support.spotify.com/vn-en/article/equalizer/ (EQ under Settings > Playback; audio settings cannot be changed while on Connect), https://www.headphonesty.com/2022/03/best-spotify-equalizer-settings/ (iOS path Home > Settings > Playback > Equalizer, in-app only), https://community.spotify.com/t5/Other-Podcasts-Partners-etc/Clicking-on-the-equalizer-for-spotify-just-brings-me-to-my-dolby/td-p/6576901 (Android may redirect to the system or Dolby EQ). Medium confidence. Dated mobile visual evidence is still needed per UI_RESEARCH.md before choosing the EQ visual contract.

| SHELL-TOOLS-WIRELESS: Home/Search header vertical-ellipsis -> legacy Tools grid -> 'Wireless' tile -> legacy Wireless grid with tiles Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, AirPlay, Transfer, SonixLink | main_menu.c:664-670 tools button (action_cb OPEN_TOOLS) -> main_menu.c:150 switch_screen(tools_screen); tools grid main_menu.c:646-655 entry {"wireless",&wireless_screen} (649) -> gridpage.c:97 switch_screen_cb -> wireless.c:16-31 gridpage_build(2x3) + settingsrow_title<br>sonix-player/src/gui/shell/main_menu.c, sonix-player/src/gui/wireless/wireless.c, sonix-player/src/gui/shell/gridpage.c | legacy | **major**: The only way into connectivity from the Spotify shell is two stacked legacy gridpage screens: the Tools grid and then the Wireless grid. Both use Adwaita cards (#303030 on #242424, radius 12, blue accent), not the reference shell. (main_menu.c:646-655 gridpage_build(tools_screen,...); wireless.c:28 gridpage_build(wireless_screen,...); gridpage.c:18 TILE_RADIUS 12 'Adwaita card radius', gridpage.c:66 theme_style_card; theme.c:21-33 palette_dark #242424/#303030/#3584e4)<br>**polish**: The comment says six entries fill the 2x3 grid, but only five are defined. **Shared components:**

- gui/shell/settingsrow.c (settingsrow_page/title/add/toggle): every connectivity page (Wi-Fi, details, AirPlay, Transfer, SonixLink, Bluetooth, BT Audio, codec list, receiver, AirPods, Press-and-hold) is built on it. Its Adwaita treatment comes from theme_style_screen #242424, theme_style_card #303030, 100px rows, radius 12, a 32px title next to the floating chevron, and Adwaita-green #2ec27e active chevrons (settingsrow.c:28,44,75-157,654-662). A reference-mode (player_layout_alt==3) variant there would restyle about 12 connectivity screens at once.
- gui/shell/theme.c palette_dark/accent (theme.c:21-33,69-77): theme()->accent defaults to #3584e4 and is used for connected/active states in wifisettings.c, btsettings.c, btaudio.c, airpodspage.c, wifitransfer.c and the published SonixLink accent. A Spotify-mode palette (#121212/#282828/#b3b3b3/#1ed760) fixes all of them.
- gui/shell/popover.c to be replaced by gui/shell/bottomsheet.c for the row menus: the Wi-Fi network menu (wifisettings.c:262,268), the BT device menu (btsettings.c:343,350) and the receiver codec chooser (btreceiverpage.c:217). bottomsheet.c is currently unused in this area.
- gui/shell/confirm.c: the Transfer leave and BT receiver leave guards use the legacy confirm card. A Spotify dialog fixes both.
- gui/shell/gui.c gui_modal_show (gui.c:361-404,443-490): the non-dismissable pairing modal. Adding an optional cancel action fixes the 90 s lock-out.
- A new shared text-entry sheet (keyboard.c + bottomsheet.c): three near-identical hand-built layers (Wi-Fi password page wifisettings.c:724-787, BT device rename btsettings.c:279-330, BT local name btaudio.c:472-522) should become one component. keyboard.c is already reference-aware (keyboard.c:30-32).
- gui/shell/gridpage.c and main_menu.c tools grid (main_menu.c:646-655) plus wireless.c: the two legacy grid hops that are the only connectivity entry from the Spotify shell. Replacing them with a Spotify Settings/Devices list removes the legacy hand-off for every connectivity route.
- gui/shell/quickpanel.c circle buttons (quickpanel.c:1316-1338): the second entry path into the area, still accent-blue legacy toggles.

**Probe/test notes:** ui_probe.c (sonix-player/src/gui/shell/ui_probe.c) has no navigation command for any connectivity screen. Its route field only distinguishes 'collection' / 'main' / 'other' (ui_probe.c:102), and its 'modal' flag reports bottomsheet_active() only, so the popover, confirm.c dialogs and the gui_modal pairing modal are not flagged (they do appear in the node walk of lv_layer_top, ui_probe.c:119). tools/test_ui_collections.py has no connectivity cases. Today these routes are reachable on host only through SDL clicks via offline_ui_session.py: tap the Home ellipsis (main_menu.c:664-670, about x=382,y=top_bar+34), then the Wireless tile in the Tools grid, then the destination tile. Missing probe commands: (1) 'route <name>' calling switch_screen() for wireless/wifisettings/btsettings/btaudio/airplay/wifitransfer/sonixlink/btreceiverpage/airpodspage, plus exported accessors for the static password_screen, details_wifi_screen (wifisettings.c:61,100), codec_screen (btaudio.c:39) and hold_screen (airpodspage.c:67); (2) the route string extended with those names; (3) a 'modal' report covering popover/confirm/gui_modal_visible() (gui.c:411-413) and back_btn visibility, which is needed to verify the forced-hidden chevron on the rename layers. Host fixtures: wifi_available() checks /sys/class/net/wlan0 (wifi.c:285-287), bluetooth_available() checks /sys/class/bluetooth or rfkill (bluetooth.c:1308-1313; may be true on a Linux host, making renders nondeterministic), airplay_available() needs the shairport binaries (airplay.c:338-340), and wifitransfer_available() needs thttpd (wifitransfer.c:745). Only AirPods has a fake (SONIX_AIRPODS_FAKE, airpods.c:774). Fakes for Wi-Fi scan results/status/op results and for BT paired/found/codec lists are needed to render populated, empty, searching, wrong-password and pairing states. Without them the host can only show the no-hardware/unavailable states. Suggested tests: the Tools->Wireless->each tile journey with back returning to Home, repeated entry/exit; Wi-Fi password journey (masked field, eye toggle, OK returns to list, history not re-showing the password page per switcher.c:455-466); BT rename layer open, leave by swipe and check the chevron restored; Transfer and receiver leave guards; while audio plays, assert queue and current file unchanged across connectivity navigation, and that receiver entry/exit restores the playback state. All radio, pairing, codec, AirPlay and transfer behaviour is hardware-only and unverified on host.

**Shared components:**

- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/popover.c: the legacy anchored popover (Adwaita #303030 card, accent-coloured checks) backs the book overflow menu, speed picker, legacy sort menus and bookmark delete. Re-skinning it to the bottomsheet.c language, or routing popover_show through a shared bottom-sheet action list, fixes AB-NP-MENU, AB-NP-SPEED, AB-BOOKMARKS-BOOK delete and AB-LEG-BOOKS sort in one change.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/settingsrow.c (settingsrow_title/settingsrow_page/pills/toggles/duration) plus theme.c palette_dark (Adwaita #242424/#303030/#3584e4): chapters, bookmarks, summary, audiobook options, change controls and scan pages all inherit theme_style_* from here. Pointing the reference-layout palette at the Spotify tokens (#121212 bg, #202124 sheet, #282828 raised, #b3b3b3 secondary, #1ed760 accent) and a Spotify header/back treatment would move about six audiobook pages, and roughly 55 other files, at once. Each page still needs auditing for hard-coded colours, e.g. audiobooks.c:1608 red Cancel.
- sonix-player/src/gui/library/homeview.c book_details(): the only Spotify-style book surface. Extending it, or a shared collection page (collection_header.c/medialist used by albums and playlists), with Chapters, Bookmarks, full description, author/series and Mark as finished would retire AB-SUMMARY, AB-LEG-BOOKS and AB-LEG-NAMES handoffs.
- sonix-player/src/gui/nowplaying/chapters.c rebuild(): convert to the 12-widget windowed pool already used in audiobooks.c (ROW_POOL/WINDOW_ROWS) to meet bounded-row constraints, and restyle it as a shared list or sheet.
- sonix-player/src/gui/library/audiobooks.c play_from()/audiobooks_play_at()/play_book(): one playback entry for books. Home should resolve through audiobook_resume_point(), and a single decision is needed on whether starting a book expands Now Playing (Home: no; legacy: yes).
- sonix-player/src/gui/nowplaying/player.c sonix_options_open / more_btn_event_cb: in audiobook mode, open a book variant of the Sonix bottom-sheet options with Chapters, Add bookmark, Bookmarks, Summary, Sleep timer and Speed instead of the popover, and hide the lyrics action for books in sonix_layout().
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/gridpage.c + main_menu.c tools_screen: the tools grid is the legacy hub. Removing the Audiobooks tile depends on relocating authors, series, finished, bookmarks index and options into shared UI first.
- sonix-player/src/gui/shell/confirm.c and toast.c: legacy confirm and toast visuals used by the rescan confirm and bookmark feedback.

**Probe/test notes:** Current coverage: tools/test_ui_collections.py exercises only --journey home-audiobooks and home-audiobooks-empty (lines 242-283): chip, shelves, details sheet, Resume at the saved position, scan isolation and category persistence. Nothing covers the book player, chapters, bookmarks, summary, speed, skip sizes, sleep/stop-at-chapter, finished handling, pagination beyond page 1, or any legacy audiobook page. Gaps in ui_probe.c: (1) The route field only distinguishes 'collection', 'main' and 'other' (ui_probe.c:102). Add names for audiobooks_screen, audiobooksettings_screen, audiobookcontrols_screen, audiobookscan_screen and chapters_screen, plus the static books_screen/names_screen in audiobooks.c and books/marks/summary screens in audiobookextras.c. Those static screens need exported getters or a probe hook, so a test can assert that no legacy route is reachable. (2) Report audiobook state: audiobook_mode / audiobook_is_playing, speed permille, skip back/forward, the current chapter index (audiobook_chapter_at), the sleeptimer AUDIOBOOK enabled/minutes, the stop_chapter_end flag and the current book key. (3) Add a 'popover' visible flag. The popover lives on lv_layer_top, which walk() already traverses (ui_probe.c:119), but there is no explicit modal flag like bottomsheet_active(). (4) The setup commands only cover collection-find, search-text, keyboard-refresh, playlist and album (ui_probe.c:76-91). Add 'player-open', 'audiobook <path> [seconds]' (calls audiobooks_play_at) and possibly 'screen <name>' for inventory captures. Fixture gaps (tools/make_ui_fixture.py:27-29): books are short FLAC copies with no chapter marks, no folder (multi-part) book, no long-duration book (hour formatting, 'Resume at' > 60 min) and fewer than 13 books, so pagination is never exercised. Add a chaptered m4b or an ID3 CHAP mp3, a folder book with 3 parts, a >1 h book and 13+ books. Suggested new journeys: - 'audiobook-player': resume from Home, open the mini-player, assert book-mode glyphs, overflow -> Chapters -> pick -> returns to the player at the chapter start; Add bookmark -> Bookmarks -> jump/delete; Summary -> back; speed 1.5x -> restart -> speed persists; music track after the book plays at 1.0. - 'audiobook-legacy-inventory': click the header ellipsis -> Audiobooks tile and capture every legacy page as gap evidence. Do not approve as a baseline. - 'audiobook-sleep': quick-panel and options sleep timer pauses and writes the position. - Repeat entry/exit while audio plays, and a restart test for config keys that are set without config_save (speed, skip, rewind, stop_chapter_end). Existing tests encode the 'Resume at 0:45' mm:ss format (test_ui_collections.py:247), which will need updating if hours or time-left formatting is added.

**Shared components:**

- gui/shell/main_menu.c tools_screen + OPEN_TOOLS (single entry; replacing it with one Spotify-style Extras list fixes XTRA-TOOLS-BTN, XTRA-TOOLS-GRID and XTRA-MORE-GRID and makes the footer/mini-player available)
- gui/shell/gridpage.c gridpage_build/add_tile (legacy tile launcher used by tools_screen and morepage_screen)
- gui/shell/settingsrow.c settingsrow_page/settingsrow_title/settingsrow_content_top (header 'back chevron + font_ui_32 title', content top 130 px, scroll-to-top on unload) and settingsrow_toggle/pills/action rows used by Files, Books, Game Boy, DAC pages; a Spotify header variant (font_ui_28 bold, PAD 20, HEADER_H 64) and reference-layout switch (player_layout_alt==3) here would convert ~all extras at once
- gui/shell/theme.c palette (#242424/#303030/blue #3584e4) and theme_style_* used by every extras page; a reference palette (#121212/#282828/#1ed760) selected when player_layout_alt==3 fixes blue switches, pills, steppers, folder glyphs and tile cards globally
- gui/shell/popover.c (anchored Adwaita popover) used by Files row menu, bookmarks delete and metronome beats: replace with gui/shell/bottomsheet.c rows
- gui/shell/toast.c, gui_notify_popup in gui/shell/gui.c, gui/shell/confirm.c, gui_modal_show: centred 60% veil cards used for every extras notice; a Spotify snackbar plus Spotify dialog would fix ~20 call sites (filespage.c, ebookreader.c, ebookmarkspage.c, gearboyplay.c, flappybird.c, metronomepage.c, dacpage.c)
- main_menu_mount_footer/main_menu_restore_footer (main_menu.c:586-597): the way to give Extras list pages a mini-player and nav footer
- gui/shell/keyboard.c keyboard_create (already Spotify-green in reference layout) used by Files name layer; only its host layer needs restyling
- gui/shell/volume_overlay.c allow_outside_player and gui.c key_async_cb: one place to fix volume HUD in reader and key routing for games
- gui/shell/switcher.c back_btn_force_hidden/topbar_set_hidden pattern used by reader, Game Boy, Flappy: needs a visible, consistent exit affordance for the full-screen canvases
- Makefile:64 -DGB_CORE=1 and morepage.c #if GB_CORE (stale conditional)

**Probe/test notes:** Current harness: tools/test_ui_collections.py drives real SDL input through tools/offline_ui_session.py (commands start, click x y, drag x1 y1 x2 y2, shot, stop) and reads state through gui/shell/ui_probe.c (request/response.json). ui_probe.c exposes a coarse route string only ('collection' / 'main' / 'other', ui_probe.c:102) plus nodes/rect/hidden/clickable/labels and bottomsheet_active(); none of the extras can be identified or asserted today. Setup commands available: collection-find, search-text, keyboard-refresh, playlist <name>, album <value>, dump. No journey in test_ui_collections.py touches tools_screen, More, Books, Game Boy, Flappy, Metronome or Files. Reaching the routes with real input (computed from code, verify with a dump): header ellipsis centre approx (382,78) (main_menu.c:662-666, x=cfg->screen_width-122, y=top_bar_height+10, 48x48) on Home or Search; gear approx (438,78). Tools grid tiles (padding 15, content top 130, tile 218x204, gap 14): music (124,247), wireless (356,247), audiobooks (124,465), more (356,465), settings (124,683). More grid same cells: dac (124,247), gearboy (356,247), books (124,465), file_explorer (356,465), flappy_bird (124,683), metronome (356,683). Back is the persistent chevron at top-left (switcher.c back_btn, ~ (43,72)) or a right swipe (drag 5,400 -> 400,400); the reader and game screens have neither (long-press centre 600 ms; Game Boy centre press (240,216); Flappy Exit bitmap button on the title). Missing probe additions needed (propose, do not implement here): (1) a 'route' field naming the active screen by pointer (tools, more, ebook-shelf, ebook-reader, ebook-bookmarks, ebook-marks, ebook-settings, gearboy-list, gearboy-play, gearboy-settings, flappy, metronome, files, textview, imageview, dac) plus layer flags for popover/confirm/toast/modal/popup veils and name/pick layers in filespage; (2) setup commands 'open <route>' (tools, more, files <path>, ebooks, ebook-open <file>, ebook-marks <file>, gearboy, gearboy-play <rom>, flappy, metronome, dac) so each can be entered deterministically; (3) a 'footer' flag reporting whether mini-player/nav are mounted on the current screen; (4) scroll offset of the main list for state-retention checks; (5) in the reader, current spine/block/offset, theme and page-turn mode; (6) in Game Boy, gearboy_running/paused/frame_count, waiting_for_state and visiting_settings; (7) audio status before/after launching Game Boy/Flappy/Metronome to assert the audio-takeover policy; (8) text of status_label/empty notes for scanning and empty states. Fixtures to add to tools/make_ui_fixture.py create(): Ebook/ with 3-4 small valid .epub files (one with TOC and cover, one without TOC, one corrupt to exercise ebookreader_open_failed, one >300-file folder variant for SHELF_MAX); an ebook.ini with bookmarks (harness already sets SONIX_EBOOK_CONFIG, offline_ui_session.py:35) including a book with zero marks; Games/GB and Games/GBC with minimal valid ROMs >=32 KB (gbdb ROM_MIN_SIZE) and Games/Saves; a folder tree for Files with .txt, .png, .epub, .gb, .flac, empty folder, 2000+ entry folder, deeply nested path. Flappy needs its resource tree under SONIX_RESOURCE_DIR/gui/flappybird. Journeys to script: (a) Home ellipsis -> tools -> more -> each tile -> back, repeated 3x, asserting route, no footer, and tab/scroll retained; (b) with audio playing, enter each extra and record state before/after (Game Boy and Flappy stop it; metronome refuses; reader keeps it) and mini-player state on return; (c) Books: open book, long-press centre, Chapters/Font/Theme, double-tap bookmark, close, assert shelf scroll, restart with --preserve-config and assert position/bookmarks/settings in ebook.ini; (d) Bookmarks: delete last mark and screenshot the blank per-book page; (e) Files: navigate, rename with Spotify keyboard, move/copy with modal (assert no cancel), delete folder and measure UI stall, tap .epub/.gb rows (expect no-op today); (f) Game Boy: list scanning overlap on re-entry, switch GB/GBC mid-scan, launch ROM, centre menu, save/load state, settings round trip and back, quit; on the simulator the LVGL pressable fallback applies (gearboyplay.c fallback_keys), one finger only; (g) Flappy: launch, exit from title, game over -> menu -> exit, missing-files popup by hiding resources; (h) Metronome: start with and without audio playing, beats popover, tap tempo, leave while running, hardware PLAY_PAUSE key. Hardware-only uncertainties: PCM contention from side keys during Game Boy/Flappy, 16 ms refresh and multitouch (gbinput evdev) on the R1, scan time/CRC on a real card, epub parsing memory on 64 MB, and audio_stop side effects on queue persistence. Host timing/memory from the simulator are not R1 measurements.

**Shared components:**

- sonix-player/src/gui/library/medialist.c reference_layout() (line 445), sonix-player/src/gui/shell/keyboard.c:30 reference_keyboard() and sonix-player/src/gui/nowplaying/trackmenu.c:1798 each re-read config player_layout_alt with default 3, separately from player.c layout_choice. Replacing them with one exported player_reference_layout(), and removing or hiding the non-Sonix layout pills in musicsettings.c:1563-1570, fixes NP-LEGACY-LAYOUTS. It also removes every '!reference_layout() -> switch_screen(player_screen)' fallback.
- Track-actions surface: medialist.c menu_btn_cb (3658-3720, popover), search.c track_long_pressed_cb (291-325, popover), trackmenu.c trackmenu_open (1889-1898, popover) and player.c sonix_options_open (4052-4064, bottom sheet). Making the player's sheet builder a shared trackactions_open(path, context) fixes COL-TRACK-MENU, SRCH-SEEALL, NP-LEGACY-LAYOUTS and the Search-tab gap with no per-result actions. That builder already provides song header, add to playlist, queue (priority), album, artists and credits.
- Add-to-playlist entry points: playlistpage_add_track, playlistpage_add_tracks and playlistpage_add_favourites (playlistpage.c:1162-1209) are called from medialist.c:1633, 1671 and 3617, search.c:238, trackmenu.c:1835, browser.c:797-800 and sonixlink.c. Routing them to collectionview_manage_track (collectionview.c:765) with a multi-path variant removes the legacy picker from all Spotify journeys. It also fixes the collections metadata loss on rename and delete (PL-LEGACY).
- Credits/details: trackmenu_details_open (trackmenu.c:1880-1886) must close the player sheet before switching, or become a bottom-sheet or reference page. build_details_page (trackmenu.c:1722-1760) needs reference tokens and the footer. This fixes NP-OPTIONS (broken route) and NP-DETAILS.
- medialist.c build_panel (3887-4060) and page_paint (4479-4510): painting every panel with reference tokens (#121212, flat rows, white text, footer mount for all panels, not only is_tracks at 3750) and building the collection_header head for LIBRARY_FILTER_NONE, MOST_PLAYED and genre track lists (album_extras_build 4968-4988) fixes COL-ALLSONGS, COL-NAMES and the history-dependent background colour. Artist rows should call medialist_open_artist (medialist.c:2765-2771).
- medialist.c play_artist (3354-3391): use playlist_set_shuffle(shuffled) instead of playlist_set_mode, which keeps the repeat mode. Remove play_menu_random_album_cb's unconditional switch_screen(player_screen) at 3466. homeview.c play() (129-149) must drop player_sheet_open(true) to keep preview mode. This fixes COL-PLAYBACK, HOME-FEED and HOME-OPTIONS.
- Queue add semantics: playlist_enqueue (playlist.c:817-825) inserts into the automatic context when queue_add_end is set. Every user 'Add to queue' (medialist.c:3627, 1648; search.c:229) should use playlist_priority_add as the swipe gestures and sheets already do.
- popover.c (theme_style_card anchored card) serves sort (medialist.c:3173), speed (player.c:3498), audiobook menu (player.c:3871), playlistpage menus and search. A bottomsheet-backed action list API with checked-state support would migrate all of them at once.
- bottomsheet.c: add a standard header (title + single close) and top-only corner radius. Callers in collectionview.c sheet_new, playlistview.c, player.c sonix_sheet_create and homeview.c then stop hand-placing duplicate Done/X controls.
- theme.c / settingsrow.c / confirm.c / toast.c / keyboard.c key styles use the Adwaita palette (#242424 screen, #303030 card, #3584e4 accent). A reference palette (screen #121212, raised #282828, sheet #202124, accent #1ed760) applied when the reference layout is active would lift the remaining legacy pages: queue title and controls, details, confirm dialog, toasts, keyboard keys and search_screen. Home and discography hardcode white text, so the light theme must either be disabled in the reference shell or the hardcoded colours must become tokens (homeview.c:52-58).
- main_menu.c action_cb (139-157): repoint OPEN_PLAYLISTS (Your Library Playlists filter), OPEN_BROWSE, OPEN_LOCAL_SEARCH (carry the query and stay in the tab), OPEN_TOOLS (tools gridpage) and OPEN_SETTINGS to Spotify-style destinations. Delete the dead OPEN_CLASSIC_LIBRARY and OPEN_WIFI.

**Probe/test notes:** ui_probe.c (sonix-player/src/gui/shell/ui_probe.c) supports only these setup commands: 'collection-find <text>', 'search-text <text>', 'keyboard-refresh', 'playlist <name>' and 'album <value>'. Its 'route' field reports only 'collection', 'main' or 'other' (ui_probe.c:101), so a legacy handoff (search_screen, details_screen, queue_screen, playlistpage_screen, browser_screen, tools_screen, chapters_screen, discography) is indistinguishable from any other page. Proposed probe extensions: (1) Report a named route for every screen pointer, including details, queue, playlistpage, search_screen, browser, tools, discography, chapters, marks, summary, settings and music. (2) Add the active screen's resolved bg colour and the top-layer modal kind (bottomsheet, popover or confirm, distinguished by checking popover and confirm visibility) so tests can assert no #242424, #303030 or #3584e4 legacy surfaces. (3) Add setup commands: 'artist <name>' (discography_open), 'home-scope <n>', 'library-filter <n>', 'now-playing' (player_sheet_open), 'np-options', 'queue-sheet', 'layout <n>' (to prove non-reference layouts are unreachable or hidden), 'liked', 'all-songs' and 'play-file <path>'. (4) Report config screen.player_layout_alt and the playlist_get_mode value in addition to shuffle and repeat. Tests to add to tools/test_ui_collections.py: (a) Now Playing > ellipsis > 'Track information' must end with route=details visible and now_playing_open=false. Today it stays open with the details page hidden (NP-OPTIONS). (b) Set repeat-all in Now Playing, open a collection and press Play. Repeat must still be 1 (COL-PLAYBACK). (c) The Home card play button and the Home options 'Play collection' must leave now_playing_open=false (HOME-FEED). (d) Search 'See all results' must keep the query and stay in the Spotify route, and a track tap must not open Now Playing (SRCH-SEEALL). (e) A collection row ellipsis must open modal=bottomsheet, not popover, and 'Add to playlist' must not change the route to playlistpage (COL-TRACK-MENU). (f) All songs must expose a collection header, Play/Shuffle and pull-to-find, with a #121212 background on first entry before any album (COL-ALLSONGS). (g) Search browse tiles Playlists, Artists, Genres and Files must not leave the Spotify route family (SRCH-BROWSE, SRCH-FILES). (h) Queue sheet: assert that no clickable node overlaps the sheet close X rect, and that tapping the X closes the sheet (NP-QUEUE). (i) With allow_delete enabled, the selection mode in the reference collection must not show a delete control. Today the FAB overlaps the mini-player (COL-SELECT). (j) Light theme: Home labels must have contrast against the page background (HOME-FEED). (k) Album header artist text must be clickable and open the artist route (COL-ALBUM). (l) The Home 'All' and 'Music' chips must render different content, or one must be removed. Constraint: all evidence above comes from reading the source. The simulator was not run (read-only task), so the rendered pixels, the light-theme contrast and the queue X overlap need probe confirmation.

## Completeness critic

### Routes missing from the subsystem passes

| ID / journey | Entry | Presentation | Findings | Required behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SHELL-SCAN-ENTRY-GAP: Fresh or empty card: how a user in the Spotify shell gets music indexed (scan, choose folders, progress, return) | Unreachable from the Spotify tabs. Only: Home/Search tools ellipsis -> Tools -> Music -> empty-index Scan panel (music.c:266-269), or Tools -> Music -> gear -> Music settings -> Scan options -> Scan music library (musicsettings.c:1066-1069). Both call libraryscan_choose_folders (libraryscan.c:410-421). | legacy | **major**: The Home 'Add music to your card to start your Home feed.' empty state can never show, because Liked Songs is always added to the Quick access grid, so item_count is never 0. With an empty index Home shows one Liked Songs card and no Scan call to action. Search and Library have no scan or empty-index state either. (homeview.c:360 (quick_add accepts LIBRARY_LIST_FAVOURITES without resolve), homeview.c:274 (card() increments item_count), homeview.c:689-690 (empty text guarded by !item_count))<br>**major**: Scan, folder chooser and progress page use the legacy centred-card veil and the full-screen scan page. ### Corrected claims

- **HOME-FEED / AB-HOME-PLAY**: The inventory presents Home Play as plain 'starts playback' and says book play 'does not expand Now Playing', but music play from the same Home DOES expand Now Playing. This contradicts UI_SPEC's preview rule and is inconsistent with book play. (homeview.c:139-148 (player_sheet_open(true) at 147) versus homeview.c:129-136; UI_SPEC.md:42-44)
- **SET-LANGUAGE**: SET-LANGUAGE says tapping a language 'applies immediately'. That holds only for legacy pages. Home, Search, Your Library, nav, sheets and collection headers use literal English and never change, so the language journey is not complete for the Spotify shell. (language.c:116-123; main_menu.c:616,704; tr( counts: homeview.c 0, collection_header.c 0, playlistview.c 0, discography.c 0, bottomsheet.c 0)
- **COL-SELECT**: COL-SELECT lists the red delete FAB neutrally, but it unlinks audio files from the collection UI. AGENTS.md says collection changes must never delete audio files, and the inventory does not flag this. (medialist.c:~1799 unlink; medialist.c:1304,1820-1840)
- **HOME-FEED empty state**: The inventory implies an empty Home message exists. It is dead code because Liked Songs is always added to the quick-access list, and no scan CTA is available in the shell. (homeview.c:360,274,689-690)
- **SET-ROOT / SET-APPEARANCE**: Both are tagged legacy, but the inventory never records that the legacy base palette is Adwaita (#242424, blue #3584e4) rather than the Spotify tokens, nor that Light theme, accent and Large text are reachable and unverified against Spotify pages. (theme.c:24-50; medialist.c:4482,4489; appearance.c:96-128)
- **SHELL-HDR-LIBSEARCH / SHELL-HDR-LIBCREATE vs LIB-SEARCH / LIB-CREATE**: The shell inventory marks Library search and create as [mixed]; the core inventory marks the same routes [spotify]. The two labels need reconciling. The sheets use the shared bottomsheet and reference keyboard, but their error popups are legacy. (collectionview.c:156-170 (bottomsheet_open), collectionview.c:~206 gui_notify_popup in save_action, keyboard.c:31)
- **XTRA-TOOLS-BTN**: Marked [mixed], but it opens the all-legacy gridpage (XTRA-TOOLS-GRID and SHELL-HDR-TOOLS are [legacy]). The classification is inconsistent. (main_menu.c:662-666, 646-655)
- **SHELL-OPEN-WIFI-DEAD**: Incomplete. OPEN_CLASSIC_LIBRARY is also an orphan action that the inventory misses. (main_menu.c:152 (no caller; all OPEN_* uses are at 435-446 and 662-667))
- **COL-NAMES**: Correctly legacy, but it omits that artist rows from these lists do not reach the discography page used everywhere else. That is a cross-route inconsistency, not just a styling issue. (medialist.c:2741-2749 versus medialist.c:3216-3217)
- **SHELL-MINI**: Tagged [spotify] without noting that the mini-player and nav are mounted only on collection and discography pages. All other secondary pages lack them. (main_menu_mount_footer callers: medialist.c:3751, discography.c:132 only)

### Research gaps

- docs/UI_RESEARCH.md has an empty Records section (lines 56-60: 'No feature research is asserted'). No URL, access date, platform or version is recorded for any route, so every '(documented)' label in the inventory is an unrecorded claim. AGENTS.md says not to mark a feature researched until its sources have been read.
- Home Play behaviour (preview versus expand Now Playing) and the Spotify Home card Play/Resume semantics have no cited evidence. The code contradicts the written preview rule, so this needs a dated mobile observation.
- Local music scan/import and empty first-run states (no card, empty index, Local Files setup, USB-shared card) have no assigned research agent. The 'Local files' research covers only the file browser.
- Sleep timer for music and the Now Playing options sheet (sleep, output switching), plus pause-on-unplug and sleep-expiry feedback, are not researched for music. Only the audiobook sleep timer is listed.
- Artist page: no Follow, Play or Shuffle controls exist, and the discography evidence cannot justify the current minimal page. Research the current Android/iOS artist page and the Search browse-all behaviour, and how Search artist results versus browse tiles reach it.
- Language and localisation (Settings > Language, behaviour of the shell when changed) is researched only for the first-run step. CJK and keyboard-layout coverage of the Spotify shell is unresearched.
- Appearance options (Light theme, accent colour, dynamic tint, text size). Spotify mobile's current theme and accessibility behaviour is not researched, so there is no basis for keeping or retiring them.
- Items already marked (inferred), (unavailable) or (community report) in the inventory remain weak: first-boot welcome/language/Wi-Fi/clock, Date and time pickers, toast/snackbar/confirm patterns, back navigation and swipe-back, Control Centre/quick panel, power menu, screensaver, low-battery modal, PEQ/MSEB/keypad, metronome, Game Boy, Flappy, E-book reader, bookmarks, AirPlay/Bluetooth receiver and AirPods pages, cross-cutting audio focus. Each needs either a source or a documented 'R1-only, no Spotify counterpart' decision.
- On-screen keyboard: Spotify mobile uses the OS keyboard, so the QWERTY/T9/Japanese keyboard, its preview/candidates and the 123 layout popover need an explicit R1-adaptation record and visual spec.
- Collection destructive actions (delete tracks, delete playlist, remove from playlist, M3U import/backup, Move to folder) lack recorded research. Spotify never deletes local files, so the allow_delete feature has no reference at all.
- Screenshot and key-lock gestures, Last.fm scrobbling account flow, USB mass-storage and Wi-Fi Transfer pages have no reference evidence or adaptation record. Hardware-only behaviour (R1 firmware, power menu, key remap, DAC states, Bluetooth codecs) stays unverified against device measurements.
